6801#[cfg(test)]
6802mod appropriated_signal_tests {
6803    use super::*;
6804
6805    /// The set is closed, and it is closed BY MEASUREMENT: every signal 1..64 was
6806    /// run under two delivery paths against a native control, and exactly these
6807    /// two differ. If a third is ever appropriated it must be added here, because
6808    /// the diagnostic is the only thing that makes the loss visible.
6809    #[test]
6810    fn the_appropriated_set_is_exactly_sigtrap_and_sigstkflt() {
6811        let signums: Vec<i32> = APPROPRIATED_SIGNALS.iter().map(|(s, _)| *s).collect();
6812        assert_eq!(signums, vec![libc::SIGTRAP, libc::SIGSTKFLT]);
6813        // SIGSTKFLT is appropriated because reverie uses it as the PMU timer, so
6814        // the two must not drift apart.
6815        assert_eq!(libc::SIGSTKFLT, reverie::PERF_EVENT_SIGNAL as i32);
6816    }
6817
6818    /// ⚠️ SIG_DFL and SIG_IGN LOSE NOTHING. A guest that is not asking to be
6819    /// called back has no expectation to disappoint, and warning there would make
6820    /// the marker noise instead of signal -- Go registers SIGSTKFLT routinely.
6821    #[test]
6822    fn only_a_real_handler_is_reported() {
6823        for signum in [libc::SIGTRAP, libc::SIGSTKFLT] {
6824            assert!(!reports(signum, 0), "SIG_DFL must not warn");
6825            assert!(!reports(signum, 1), "SIG_IGN must not warn");
6826            assert!(reports(signum, 0x4000_1234), "a real handler must warn");
6827        }
6828    }
6829
6830    /// An ordinary signal is delivered normally and must never be reported.
6831    #[test]
6832    fn an_unappropriated_signal_is_never_reported() {
6833        for signum in [libc::SIGUSR1, libc::SIGTERM, libc::SIGINT, 10, 30] {
6834            assert!(
6835                !reports(signum, 0x4000_1234),
6836                "signal {signum} is not appropriated"
6837            );
6838        }
6839    }
6840
6841    /// Calls the REAL predicate. Deliberately not a re-implementation: a
6842    /// mirrored copy would keep passing if `appropriated_reason` were gutted.
6843    fn reports(signum: i32, handler: u64) -> bool {
6844        appropriated_reason(signum, handler).is_some()
6845    }
6846}

6848#[cfg(test)]
6849mod rt_sigsuspend_tests {
6850    use reverie::GlobalRPC;
6851    use reverie::GlobalTool;
6852    use reverie::Pid;
6853    use reverie::Tool;
6854    use reverie::syscalls::LocalMemory;
6855    use reverie::syscalls::Syscall;
6856    use reverie::syscalls::SyscallInfo;
6857
6858    use super::*;
6859    use crate::Config;
6860    use crate::GlobalState;
6861    use crate::ThreadState;
6862    use crate::syscalls::threads::kernel_sigset_bit;
6863
6864    /// The guest's stack, in this process's memory: `regs` reports a stack
6865    /// pointer `STACK_WORD` words in, so the cells below its red zone are words
6866    /// `COPY_WORD - 1` (the pending set) and `COPY_WORD` (the copy).
6867    const ARENA_WORDS: usize = 64;
6868    const STACK_WORD: usize = 48;
6869    const COPY_WORD: usize = STACK_WORD - STACK_RED_ZONE / 8 - 1;
6870
6871    /// rt_sigsuspend writes its cells directly; it takes no scratch stack.
6872    struct NoStack;
6873
6874    struct NoStackGuard;
6875
6876    impl Drop for NoStackGuard {
6877        fn drop(&mut self) {}
6878    }
6879
6880    impl reverie::Stack for NoStack {
6881        type StackGuard = NoStackGuard;
6882
6883        fn size(&self) -> usize {
6884            panic!("rt_sigsuspend takes no scratch stack")
6885        }
6886        fn capacity(&self) -> usize {
6887            panic!("rt_sigsuspend takes no scratch stack")
6888        }
6889        fn push<'stack, T>(&mut self, _: T) -> Addr<'stack, T> {
6890            panic!("rt_sigsuspend takes no scratch stack")
6891        }
6892        fn reserve<'stack, T>(&mut self) -> AddrMut<'stack, T> {
6893            panic!("rt_sigsuspend takes no scratch stack")
6894        }
6895        fn commit(self) -> Result<Self::StackGuard, Errno> {
6896            panic!("rt_sigsuspend takes no scratch stack")
6897        }
6898    }
6899
6900    /// A guest whose `rt_sigsuspend` caller's mask lives at `caller_mask`. The
6901    /// call's `rt_sigpending` probe, which runs after Detcore read that mask
6902    /// and before the real call, rewrites it to `rewritten` if one is given,
6903    /// as another process sharing the buffer could while the call waits for
6904    /// its turn. Every mask a real `rt_sigsuspend` is given is kept in
6905    /// `suspended_under`. `stack_pointer`, when given, replaces the arena's
6906    /// stack pointer in `regs`.
6907    struct SuspendGuest {
6908        config: Config,
6909        thread: ThreadState<()>,
6910        arena: Box<[u64; ARENA_WORDS]>,
6911        stack_pointer: Option<usize>,
6912        caller_mask: usize,
6913        rewritten: Option<KernelSigset>,
6914        suspended_under: Vec<KernelSigset>,
6915    }
6916
6917    #[reverie::tool]
6918    impl GlobalRPC<GlobalState> for SuspendGuest {
6919        async fn send_rpc(
6920            &self,
6921            message: <GlobalState as GlobalTool>::Request,
6922        ) -> <GlobalState as GlobalTool>::Response {
6923            panic!(
6924                "an unsequentialized rt_sigsuspend sends no RPC: {:?}",
6925                message.2
6926            )
6927        }
6928        fn config(&self) -> &Config {
6929            &self.config
6930        }
6931    }
6932
6933    #[reverie::tool]
6934    impl Guest<Detcore> for SuspendGuest {
6935        type Memory = LocalMemory;
6936        type Stack = NoStack;
6937
6938        fn tid(&self) -> Pid {
6939            Pid::from_raw(1)
6940        }
6941        fn pid(&self) -> Pid {
6942            Pid::from_raw(1)
6943        }
6944        fn ppid(&self) -> Option<Pid> {
6945            None
6946        }
6947        fn memory(&self) -> Self::Memory {
6948            LocalMemory::new()
6949        }
6950        fn thread_state_mut(&mut self) -> &mut ThreadState<()> {
6951            &mut self.thread
6952        }
6953        fn thread_state(&self) -> &ThreadState<()> {
6954            &self.thread
6955        }
6956        async fn regs(&mut self) -> libc::user_regs_struct {
6957            // SAFETY: user_regs_struct is plain integers; all zeroes is valid.
6958            let mut regs: libc::user_regs_struct = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
6959            regs.rsp = self
6960                .stack_pointer
6961                .unwrap_or(self.arena.as_ptr() as usize + STACK_WORD * 8)
6962                as u64;
6963            regs
6964        }
6965        async fn stack(&mut self) -> Self::Stack {
6966            NoStack
6967        }
6968        async fn daemonize(&mut self) {
6969            panic!("rt_sigsuspend must not daemonize")
6970        }
6971        async fn inject<S: SyscallInfo>(&mut self, syscall: S) -> Result<i64, Errno> {
6972            let (number, args) = syscall.into_parts();
6973            match Syscall::from_raw(number, args) {
6974                // read_kernel_sigset's validation probe.
6975                Syscall::RtSigprocmask(call) if call.how() == -1 => Err(Errno::EINVAL),
6976                Syscall::RtSigpending(call) => {
6977                    let set = call.set().expect("rt_sigpending without a set");
6978                    LocalMemory::new().write_value(set.cast::<KernelSigset>(), &0)?;
6979                    if let Some(rewritten) = self.rewritten {
6980                        // SAFETY: `caller_mask` is the test's live, aligned buffer.
6981                        unsafe {
6982                            std::ptr::write(self.caller_mask as *mut KernelSigset, rewritten)
6983                        };
6984                    }
6985                    Ok(0)
6986                }
6987                Syscall::RtSigsuspend(call) => {
6988                    let mask = call.mask().expect("rt_sigsuspend without a mask");
6989                    self.suspended_under
6990                        .push(LocalMemory::new().read_value(mask.cast::<KernelSigset>())?);
6991                    Err(Errno::EINTR)
6992                }
6993                other => panic!("rt_sigsuspend injected {other:?}"),
6994            }
6995        }
6996        async fn tail_inject<S: SyscallInfo>(&mut self, _: S) -> reverie::Never {
6997            panic!("rt_sigsuspend must not retire the guest")
6998        }
6999        fn set_timer(&mut self, _: reverie::TimerSchedule) -> Result<(), Error> {
7000            panic!("rt_sigsuspend must not set a timer")
7001        }
7002        fn set_timer_precise(&mut self, _: reverie::TimerSchedule) -> Result<(), Error> {
7003            panic!("rt_sigsuspend must not set a timer")
7004        }
7005        fn read_clock(&mut self) -> Result<u64, Error> {
7006            panic!("rt_sigsuspend must not read a clock")
7007        }
7008    }
7009
7010    /// The real `rt_sigsuspend` sleeps under the mask Detcore read when it
7011    /// handled the call, the one the scheduler is given, even if the guest's
7012    /// buffer changes before the call runs. Linux copies the mask when the call
7013    /// starts, and from the guest's view it has started. Before, the call ran
7014    /// with the guest's pointer: another process sharing the buffer could make
7015    /// it block a signal the scheduler had counted on to end it, and the
7016    /// scheduler would hold every other thread waiting for that wake.
7017    #[tokio::test(flavor = "current_thread")]
7018    async fn rt_sigsuspend_sleeps_under_the_mask_detcore_read() {
7019        let config = Config::default();
7020        assert!(!config.sequentialize_threads);
7021        let tool = <Detcore as Tool>::new(Pid::from_raw(1), &config);
7022        let unblocks_sigchld: KernelSigset = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGUSR2);
7023        let blocks_sigchld = unblocks_sigchld | kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGCHLD);
7024        let mut caller_mask: Box<KernelSigset> = Box::new(unblocks_sigchld);
7025        let caller_address = &mut *caller_mask as *mut KernelSigset as usize;
7026        let mut guest = SuspendGuest {
7027            thread: ThreadState::new(DetPid::from_raw(1), &config, ()),
7028            config: config.clone(),
7029            arena: Box::new([u64::MAX; ARENA_WORDS]),
7030            stack_pointer: None,
7031            caller_mask: caller_address,
7032            rewritten: Some(blocks_sigchld),
7033            suspended_under: Vec::new(),
7034        };
7035        let call = syscalls::RtSigsuspend::new()
7036            .with_mask(Addr::from_raw(caller_address))
7037            .with_sigsetsize(KERNEL_SIGSET_SIZE);
7038
7039        let result = tool.handle_rt_sigsuspend(&mut guest, call).await;
7040        assert!(
7041            matches!(result, Err(Error::Errno(Errno::EINTR))),
7042            "{result:?}"
7043        );
7044        assert_eq!(*caller_mask, blocks_sigchld, "the buffer was rewritten");
7045        assert_eq!(guest.suspended_under, [unblocks_sigchld]);
7046    }
7047
7048    /// A caller's mask that lies on the cells Detcore places below the red
7049    /// zone, as a raw syscall may pass one there in a shared stack: the copy
7050    /// must not be the caller's buffer, or a rewrite of the shared buffer
7051    /// reaches the real call again, and the rt_sigpending cell must not
7052    /// overwrite it. The cells move below the buffer, which keeps its own
7053    /// contents. Before, the copy slot was the caller's buffer, and the
7054    /// pending slot's buffer was overwritten with the pending set.
7055    #[tokio::test(flavor = "current_thread")]
7056    async fn rt_sigsuspend_scratch_never_lands_on_the_callers_mask() {
7057        let config = Config::default();
7058        let tool = <Detcore as Tool>::new(Pid::from_raw(1), &config);
7059        let original: KernelSigset = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGUSR2);
7060        let rewritten = original | kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGCHLD);
7061        // The copy's slot, rewritten by a peer; then the pending cell's slot,
7062        // which nobody rewrites.
7063        for (slot, peer_rewrite) in [(COPY_WORD, Some(rewritten)), (COPY_WORD - 1, None)] {
7064            let mut arena = Box::new([u64::MAX; ARENA_WORDS]);
7065            arena[slot] = original;
7066            let caller_address = &mut arena[slot] as *mut u64 as usize;
7067            let mut guest = SuspendGuest {
7068                thread: ThreadState::new(DetPid::from_raw(1), &config, ()),
7069                config: config.clone(),
7070                arena,
7071                stack_pointer: None,
7072                caller_mask: caller_address,
7073                rewritten: peer_rewrite,
7074                suspended_under: Vec::new(),
7075            };
7076            let call = syscalls::RtSigsuspend::new()
7077                .with_mask(Addr::from_raw(caller_address))
7078                .with_sigsetsize(KERNEL_SIGSET_SIZE);
7079
7080            let result = tool.handle_rt_sigsuspend(&mut guest, call).await;
7081            assert!(
7082                matches!(result, Err(Error::Errno(Errno::EINTR))),
7083                "slot {slot}: {result:?}"
7084            );
7085            assert_eq!(guest.suspended_under, [original], "slot {slot}");
7086            assert_eq!(
7087                guest.arena[slot],
7088                peer_rewrite.unwrap_or(original),
7089                "slot {slot}: the caller's buffer holds only what the guest wrote"
7090            );
7091        }
7092    }
7093
7094    /// A stack pointer that leaves no room for the red zone and the two cells
7095    /// below it fails the call with EFAULT, as a cell at address zero or on
7096    /// unwritable memory does, and the real call never runs. Rows: a stack
7097    /// pointer of 0 and one inside the red zone's size (no room for the red
7098    /// zone on x86_64; aarch64 has none), the red zone's bottom 0, 8 and 15
7099    /// bytes above address zero (no room for the cells), and 16 bytes above it
7100    /// (the cells would start at address zero, which is refused as null).
7101    /// Mutation "an unchecked subtraction for the cells": the rows 0, 8 and 15
7102    /// bytes above the red zone's bottom overflow, which panics in a debug
7103    /// build.
7104    #[tokio::test(flavor = "current_thread")]
7105    async fn rt_sigsuspend_with_no_room_below_the_red_zone_fails_with_efault() {
7106        let config = Config::default();
7107        let tool = <Detcore as Tool>::new(Pid::from_raw(1), &config);
7108        let mut caller_mask: Box<KernelSigset> = Box::new(kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGUSR2));
7109        let caller_address = &mut *caller_mask as *mut KernelSigset as usize;
7110        let rows = [
7111            0,
7112            STACK_RED_ZONE / 2,
7113            STACK_RED_ZONE,
7114            STACK_RED_ZONE + 8,
7115            STACK_RED_ZONE + 15,
7116            STACK_RED_ZONE + 16,
7117        ];
7118        for stack_pointer in rows {
7119            let mut guest = SuspendGuest {
7120                thread: ThreadState::new(DetPid::from_raw(1), &config, ()),
7121                config: config.clone(),
7122                arena: Box::new([u64::MAX; ARENA_WORDS]),
7123                stack_pointer: Some(stack_pointer),
7124                caller_mask: caller_address,
7125                rewritten: None,
7126                suspended_under: Vec::new(),
7127            };
7128            let call = syscalls::RtSigsuspend::new()
7129                .with_mask(Addr::from_raw(caller_address))
7130                .with_sigsetsize(KERNEL_SIGSET_SIZE);
7131
7132            let result = tool.handle_rt_sigsuspend(&mut guest, call).await;
7133            assert!(
7134                matches!(result, Err(Error::Errno(Errno::EFAULT))),
7135                "stack pointer {stack_pointer}: {result:?}"
7136            );
7137            assert!(
7138                guest.suspended_under.is_empty(),
7139                "stack pointer {stack_pointer}"
7140            );
7141        }
7142    }
7143}

7146mod before_park_tests {

7187    /// A guest whose thread is a real stopped process, so `sleep_signal`

7190    struct SleeperGuest<'a> {

7258
7259    extern "C" fn caught(_: libc::c_int) {}
7260
7261    /// `waitpid` for exactly `child`, retried on EINTR: the pid and status
7262    /// it reported, or the errno of its failure.
7263    fn wait_for(
7264        child: libc::pid_t,
7265        options: libc::c_int,
7266    ) -> Result<(libc::pid_t, libc::c_int), i32> {
7267        let mut status = 0;
7268        loop {
7269            // SAFETY: waiting for our own child.
7270            let waited = unsafe { libc::waitpid(child, &mut status, options) };
7271            if waited != -1 {
7272                return Ok((waited, status));
7273            }
7274            match std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error() {
7275                Some(libc::EINTR) => continue,
7276                errno => return Err(errno.unwrap_or(0)),
7277            }
7278        }
7279    }
7280
7281    /// The parent's ownership of the forked child: it is killed and reaped
7282    /// when the owner is dropped, so a panic or a cancelled await in the test
7283    /// leaves no process behind. `finish` does the same on the normal path
7284    /// and checks that the child was reaped.
7285    struct ChildOwner {
7286        pid: libc::pid_t,
7287        reaped: bool,
7288    }
7289
7290    impl ChildOwner {
7291        /// Wait for the child to stop. A wait that reports it exited or
7292        /// killed reaped it, so the owner is disarmed before anything can
7293        /// unwind: its pid may already belong to another process.
7294        /// An ECHILD means the child is no longer ours to wait for (it was
7295        /// reaped already), so it disarms the owner too.
7296        fn wait_stopped(&mut self) -> Result<(), Result<(libc::pid_t, libc::c_int), i32>> {
7297            let waited = wait_for(self.pid, libc::WUNTRACED);
7298            match waited {
7299                Ok((pid, status))
7300                    if pid == self.pid
7301                        && (libc::WIFEXITED(status) || libc::WIFSIGNALED(status)) =>
7302                {
7303                    self.reaped = true;
7304                }
7305                Err(libc::ECHILD) => self.reaped = true,
7306                _ => {}
7307            }
7308            match waited {
7309                Ok((pid, status)) if pid == self.pid && libc::WIFSTOPPED(status) => Ok(()),
7310                other => Err(other),
7311            }
7312        }
7313
7314        /// Whether the owner had disarmed itself, read once the child is
7315        /// known to be reaped. The owner is disarmed from here on whatever
7316        /// the answer, so a failed check of it unwinds without signalling a
7317        /// pid that may have been reused.
7318        fn disarmed_after_reap(&mut self) -> bool {
7319            std::mem::replace(&mut self.reaped, true)
7320        }
7321
7322        fn kill_and_reap(&mut self) -> Result<(libc::pid_t, libc::c_int), i32> {
7323            // SAFETY: killing our own child, which is not yet reaped.
7324            unsafe { libc::kill(self.pid, libc::SIGKILL) };
7325            let reaped = wait_for(self.pid, 0);
7326            self.reaped = true;
7327            reaped
7328        }
7329
7330        fn finish(mut self) {
7331            let reaped = self.kill_and_reap();
7332            assert!(
7333                matches!(reaped, Ok((pid, status)) if pid == self.pid
7334                    && libc::WIFSIGNALED(status)
7335                    && libc::WTERMSIG(status) == libc::SIGKILL),
7336                "the child was not reaped: {reaped:?}"
7337            );
7338        }
7339    }
7340
7341    impl Drop for ChildOwner {
7342        fn drop(&mut self) {
7343            if !self.reaped {
7344                // Unwinding already: the reap's outcome is not checked here.
7345                let _ = self.kill_and_reap();
7346            }
7347        }
7348    }
7349
7350    /// A forked child, owned by the parent from the moment `fork` returns,
7351    /// that exits with status 7 at once if `exits`.
7352    fn forked_child(exits: bool) -> ChildOwner {
7353        // SAFETY: the child calls only async-signal-safe functions, with no
7354        // allocation, lock or destructor, then stops until it is killed.
7355        let child = unsafe { libc::fork() };
7356        if child == 0 {
7357            unsafe {
7358                if exits {
7359                    libc::_exit(7);
7360                }
7361                let mut action: libc::sigaction = std::mem::zeroed();
7362                action.sa_sigaction = caught as extern "C" fn(libc::c_int) as usize;
7363                libc::sigaction(libc::SIGCHLD, &action, std::ptr::null_mut());
7364                let mut set: libc::sigset_t = std::mem::zeroed();
7365                libc::sigemptyset(&mut set);
7366                libc::sigaddset(&mut set, libc::SIGCHLD);
7367                libc::pthread_sigmask(libc::SIG_UNBLOCK, &set, std::ptr::null_mut());
7368                libc::raise(libc::SIGSTOP);
7369                libc::_exit(0);
7370            }
7371        }
7372        assert!(child > 0, "fork failed");
7373        ChildOwner {
7374            pid: child,
7375            reaped: false,
7376        }
7377    }
7378
7379    /// A child stopped as a sleeper is when Detcore handles its call before
7380    /// filing the sleep: SIGCHLD caught and unblocked, and SIGCHLD pending in
7381    /// its private queue, sent to the thread while it was stopped.
7382    fn stopped_child_with_a_private_sigchld() -> ChildOwner {
7383        let mut owner = forked_child(false);
7384        let child = owner.pid;
7385        if let Err(waited) = owner.wait_stopped() {
7386            panic!("the child did not stop: {waited:?}");
7387        }
7388        // SAFETY: the child is ours and stopped; SIGCHLD stays pending.
7389        let sent = unsafe { libc::syscall(libc::SYS_tgkill, child, child, libc::SIGCHLD) };
7390        assert_eq!(sent, 0, "tgkill failed");
7391        owner
7392    }
7393
7394    /// The before-park question through the production path: a guest's
7395    /// thread-directed SIGCHLD, committed (`PrivateSent`, as the sender's turn
7396    /// records it) while no sleep was filed, so that turn had no sleep to
7397    /// wake, is found by `sleep_signal(BeforePark)`'s own query of the ledger,
7398    /// and the sleep ends at once as pending for it. With no committed record
7399    /// the same kernel state, which `/proc` cannot tell from a host-timed copy,
7400    /// ends nothing (the Codex re-check of
7401    /// https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/pull/4177).
7402    #[tokio::test(flavor = "current_thread")]
7403    async fn a_private_sigchld_committed_before_the_sleep_is_filed_ends_it_through_the_ledger() {
7404        // The fixture's owner: a child that exits instead of stopping is
7405        // reaped by the wait, which disarms the owner, so an unwind then
7406        // signals no pid that may have been reused.
7407        let mut exited = forked_child(true);
7408        let wait = exited.wait_stopped();
7409        let exit_reaped = matches!(wait, Err(Ok((pid, status))) if pid == exited.pid
7410            && libc::WIFEXITED(status)
7411            && libc::WEXITSTATUS(status) == 7);
7412        let disarmed = exit_reaped && exited.disarmed_after_reap();
7413        assert!(exit_reaped, "{wait:?}");
7414        assert!(disarmed, "a reaped child disarms its owner");
7415        drop(exited);
7416        // A child already reaped elsewhere answers ECHILD, which disarms the
7417        // owner as well. The raw wait that reaps it disarms the owner before
7418        // anything is checked; the owner is armed again only for the call
7419        // under test.
7420        let mut gone = forked_child(true);
7421        let reaped_elsewhere = wait_for(gone.pid, 0);
7422        gone.reaped = matches!(reaped_elsewhere, Ok((pid, _)) if pid == gone.pid)
7423            || reaped_elsewhere == Err(libc::ECHILD);
7424        assert!(
7425            matches!(reaped_elsewhere, Ok((pid, _)) if pid == gone.pid),
7426            "{reaped_elsewhere:?}"
7427        );
7428        gone.reaped = false;
7429        let wait = gone.wait_stopped();
7430        let disarmed = gone.disarmed_after_reap();
7431        assert_eq!(wait, Err(Err(libc::ECHILD)));
7432        assert!(disarmed, "an ECHILD disarms its owner");
7433        drop(gone);
7434
7435        let owner = stopped_child_with_a_private_sigchld();
7436        let child = owner.pid;
7437        let state_of_child = crate::syscalls::threads::read_thread_wait_signal_state(
7438            Pid::from_raw(child),
7439            Pid::from_raw(child),
7440        )
7441        .expect("the child's signal state");
7442        let chld = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGCHLD);
7443        assert_eq!(
7444            state_of_child.thread_pending & chld,
7445            chld,
7446            "pending, private"
7447        );
7448        assert_eq!(state_of_child.blocked & chld, 0, "unblocked");
7449        assert_eq!(state_of_child.caught & chld, chld, "caught");
7450        let mut verdicts = Vec::new();
7451        for committed in [true, false] {
7452            let config = Config {
7453                sequentialize_threads: true,
7454                ..Config::default()
7455            }
7456            .with_backend(|backend| {
7457                detcore_model::config::set_kernel_signal_facts(backend, true);
7458                backend.delivers_preemption_timer_events = false;
7459                backend.runs_guest_code_between_injections = false;
7460            });
7461            let state = GlobalState::for_tests(&config);
7462            let mut guest = SleeperGuest {
7463                state: &state,
7464                thread: ThreadState::new(DetPid::from_raw(child), &config, ()),
7465                config,
7466                pid: Pid::from_raw(child),
7467            };
7468            if committed {
7469                let target = guest.thread_state().dettid;
7470                crate::tool_global::child_exit_sigchld(
7471                    &mut guest,
7472                    ChildExitSigchldControl::PrivateSent { target },
7473                )
7474                .await;
7475            }
7476            verdicts.push(sleep_signal(&mut guest, SleepCheck::BeforePark, false).await);
7477        }
7478        owner.finish();
7479        assert!(
7480            matches!(verdicts[0], Ok(SleepSignal::Pending)),
7481            "committed: {:?}",
7482            verdicts[0]
7483        );
7484        assert!(
7485            matches!(verdicts[1], Ok(SleepSignal::None)),
7486            "not committed: {:?}",
7487            verdicts[1]
7488        );
7489    }
7490
7491    /// The futex counterpart: a precise futex wait's own check before it
7492    /// parks (`futex_wait_pending_signals`) finds a guest's thread-directed
7493    /// SIGCHLD committed (`PrivateSent`) before the wait was filed, through
7494    /// the production query of the ledger, and the wait ends with its restart
7495    /// errno. With no record, or when a host-timed source can post SIGCHLD to
7496    /// the process, the same kernel state ends nothing and the wait parks
7497    /// (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/4132).
7498    #[tokio::test(flavor = "current_thread")]
7499    async fn a_private_sigchld_committed_before_a_futex_wait_parks_ends_it_through_the_ledger() {
7500        let owner = stopped_child_with_a_private_sigchld();
7501        let child = owner.pid;
7502        let state = crate::syscalls::threads::read_thread_wait_signal_state(
7503            Pid::from_raw(child),
7504            Pid::from_raw(child),
7505        )
7506        .expect("the child's signal state");
7507        let chld = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGCHLD);
7508        let interrupting = state.pending_interrupting(state.blocked, false);
7509        let mut pending = Vec::new();
7510        for (committed, host_timed) in [(true, 0), (false, 0), (true, chld)] {
7511            let config = Config {
7512                sequentialize_threads: true,
7513                ..Config::default()
7514            }
7515            .with_backend(|backend| {
7516                detcore_model::config::set_kernel_signal_facts(backend, true);
7517                backend.delivers_preemption_timer_events = false;
7518                backend.runs_guest_code_between_injections = false;
7519            });
7520            let global = GlobalState::for_tests(&config);
7521            let mut guest = SleeperGuest {
7522                state: &global,
7523                thread: ThreadState::new(DetPid::from_raw(child), &config, ()),
7524                config,
7525                pid: Pid::from_raw(child),
7526            };
7527            if committed {
7528                let target = guest.thread_state().dettid;
7529                crate::tool_global::child_exit_sigchld(
7530                    &mut guest,
7531                    ChildExitSigchldControl::PrivateSent { target },
7532                )
7533                .await;
7534            }
7535            let held = crate::syscalls::threads::futex_wait_held_after_wake(host_timed, 0);
7536            pending.push(
7537                crate::syscalls::threads::futex_wait_pending_signals(
7538                    &mut guest,
7539                    &state,
7540                    interrupting,
7541                    held,
7542                    host_timed,
7543                    0,
7544                )
7545                .await,
7546            );
7547        }
7548        owner.finish();
7549        assert_eq!(interrupting & chld, chld, "pending, unblocked and caught");
7550        assert_eq!(
7551            pending,
7552            vec![chld, 0, 0],
7553            "recorded, unrecorded, host-timed"
7554        );
7555    }
7556
7557    /// A futex wait's own check after a wake decides a held `SIGCHLD` from
7558    /// the private queue and the schedule's record together: after a flush
7559    /// cleared the record, a `SIGCHLD` only in the shared queue (which a
7560    /// host-timed source may have posted) ends nothing, and neither does a
7561    /// record whose copy is no longer in the private queue. Both waits park
7562    /// again (the design check of https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/4132).
7563    #[tokio::test(flavor = "current_thread")]
7564    async fn a_futex_waits_check_after_a_wake_needs_the_private_copy_and_its_record() {
7565        let chld = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGCHLD);
7566        let state = |thread: KernelSigset, shared: KernelSigset| {
7567            crate::syscalls::threads::KernelSignalState {
7568                pending: thread | shared,
7569                thread_pending: thread,
7570                shared_pending: shared,
7571                caught: chld,
7572                ..Default::default()
7573            }
7574        };
7575        let mut answers = Vec::new();
7576        for (name, kernel, recorded, flushed) in [
7577            ("private, recorded", state(chld, 0), true, false),
7578            ("flushed, shared only", state(0, chld), true, true),
7579            ("recorded, shared only", state(0, chld), true, false),
7580        ] {
7581            let config = Config {
7582                sequentialize_threads: true,
7583                ..Config::default()
7584            }
7585            .with_backend(|backend| {
7586                detcore_model::config::set_kernel_signal_facts(backend, true);
7587                backend.delivers_preemption_timer_events = false;
7588                backend.runs_guest_code_between_injections = false;
7589            });
7590            let global = GlobalState::for_tests(&config);
7591            let mut guest = SleeperGuest {
7592                state: &global,
7593                thread: ThreadState::new(DetPid::from_raw(4242), &config, ()),
7594                config,
7595                pid: Pid::from_raw(4242),
7596            };
7597            let target = guest.thread_state().dettid;
7598            if recorded {
7599                crate::tool_global::child_exit_sigchld(
7600                    &mut guest,
7601                    ChildExitSigchldControl::PrivateSent { target },
7602                )
7603                .await;
7604            }
7605            if flushed {
7606                crate::tool_global::child_exit_sigchld(
7607                    &mut guest,
7608                    ChildExitSigchldControl::Flushed,
7609                )
7610                .await;
7611            }
7612            let held = crate::syscalls::threads::futex_wait_held_after_wake(0, 0);
7613            let interrupting = kernel.pending_interrupting(kernel.blocked, false);
7614            let pending = crate::syscalls::threads::futex_wait_pending_signals(
7615                &mut guest,
7616                &kernel,
7617                interrupting,
7618                held,
7619                0,
7620                0,
7621            )
7622            .await;
7623            answers.push((name, pending));
7624        }
7625        assert_eq!(
7626            answers,
7627            vec![
7628                ("private, recorded", chld),
7629                ("flushed, shared only", 0),
7630                ("recorded, shared only", 0),
7631            ]
7632        );
7633    }
7634
7635    /// After the drain woke a futex waiter for a guest's private `SIGCHLD`,
7636    /// a sibling flushed it (`SIG_IGN`, then the handler again) and sent
7637    /// `kill(getpid(), SIGCHLD)`, a copy in the shared queue. Here a forked
7638    /// child, stopped with `SIGCHLD` caught and unblocked, is sent that shared
7639    /// copy, so `/proc` shows it in `ShdPnd` only, and the ledger records the
7640    /// private send and the flush. The waiter's check, as
7641    /// `handle_futex_blocking` runs it with the drain's `SIGCHLD` left out of
7642    /// the committed set (`drain_woken_waiters`), ends nothing, and the
7643    /// wait parks again: a futex wait holds shared copies. Had the drain's
7644    /// `SIGCHLD` been committed, as before the design check of
7645    /// https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/4132, the shared copy, which
7646    /// a host-timed child exit could equally have posted, would have ended it.
7647    #[tokio::test(flavor = "current_thread")]
7648    async fn a_shared_sigchld_after_a_flush_does_not_end_a_drain_woken_futex_wait() {
7649        let mut owner = forked_child(false);
7650        let child = owner.pid;
7651        if let Err(waited) = owner.wait_stopped() {
7652            panic!("the child did not stop: {waited:?}");
7653        }
7654        // SAFETY: the child is ours and stopped; SIGCHLD stays pending.
7655        assert_eq!(
7656            unsafe { libc::kill(child, libc::SIGCHLD) },
7657            0,
7658            "kill failed"
7659        );
7660        let state = crate::syscalls::threads::read_thread_wait_signal_state(
7661            Pid::from_raw(child),
7662            Pid::from_raw(child),
7663        )
7664        .expect("the child's signal state");
7665        let chld = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGCHLD);
7666        let mut pending = Vec::new();
7667        for committed_wake in [0, chld] {
7668            let config = Config {
7669                sequentialize_threads: true,
7670                ..Config::default()
7671            }
7672            .with_backend(|backend| {
7673                detcore_model::config::set_kernel_signal_facts(backend, true);
7674                backend.delivers_preemption_timer_events = false;
7675                backend.runs_guest_code_between_injections = false;
7676            });
7677            let global = GlobalState::for_tests(&config);
7678            let mut guest = SleeperGuest {
7679                state: &global,
7680                thread: ThreadState::new(DetPid::from_raw(child), &config, ()),
7681                config,
7682                pid: Pid::from_raw(child),
7683            };
7684            let target = guest.thread_state().dettid;
7685            crate::tool_global::child_exit_sigchld(
7686                &mut guest,
7687                ChildExitSigchldControl::PrivateSent { target },
7688            )
7689            .await;
7690            crate::tool_global::child_exit_sigchld(&mut guest, ChildExitSigchldControl::Flushed)
7691                .await;
7692            let held = crate::syscalls::threads::futex_wait_held_after_wake(0, committed_wake);
7693            pending.push(
7694                crate::syscalls::threads::futex_wait_pending_signals(
7695                    &mut guest,
7696                    &state,
7697                    state.pending_interrupting(state.blocked, false),
7698                    held,
7699                    0,
7700                    committed_wake,
7701                )
7702                .await,
7703            );
7704        }
7705        owner.finish();
7706        assert_eq!(
7707            (state.thread_pending & chld, state.shared_pending & chld),
7708            (0, chld),
7709            "SIGCHLD pending in the shared queue only"
7710        );
7711        assert_eq!(pending, vec![0, chld], "stripped, then committed as before");
7712    }
7713}

7715#[cfg(test)]
7716mod own_process_signal_tests {
7717    use super::*;
7718
7719    const SENDER_SIBLING: i32 = 11;
7720
7721    /// The main thread sends SIGUSR1, which it catches, to its own process
7722    /// while one other thread is parked with an empty mask: admitted (S1).
7723    fn admitted_s1() -> OwnProcessSignal {
7724        OwnProcessSignal {
7725            sig: libc::SIGUSR1,
7726            names_sender: true,
7727            caught: kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGUSR1),
7728            inputs: OwnProcessSignalInputs {
7729                siblings: vec![(
7730                    DetTid::from_raw(SENDER_SIBLING),
7731                    SiblingSignalState::InSchedule(Some(0)),
7732                )],
7733                ..Default::default()
7734            },
7735            ..Default::default()
7736        }
7737    }
7738
7739    fn refusal(edit: impl FnOnce(&mut OwnProcessSignal)) -> Option<OwnProcessSignalRefusal> {
7740        let mut send = admitted_s1();
7741        edit(&mut send);
7742        own_process_signal_refusal(&send)
7743    }
7744
7745    /// Round-3 ruling 3: a copy to user memory faults on a byte without
7746    /// write permission (read-only, execute-only, `PROT_NONE`) or in a hole,
7747    /// and not across writable mappings or below a grow-down one.
7748    #[test]
7749    fn a_copy_to_user_memory_faults_where_no_mapping_is_writable() {
7750        let smaps = "1000-2000 rw-p 0 0:0 0\nVmFlags: rd wr mr mw\n\
7751                     2000-3000 -w-p 0 0:0 0\nVmFlags: wr mw\n\
7752                     3000-4000 r--p 0 0:0 0\nVmFlags: rd mr\n\
7753                     6000-7000 --xp 0 0:0 0 [vvar]\nVmFlags: ex\n\
7754                     9000-a000 rw-p 0 0:0 0 [stack]\nVmFlags: rd wr gd\n";
7755        let mappings = parse_smaps(smaps);
7756        assert!(!user_unwritable(&mappings, 0x1fc0, 128), "rw- into -w-");
7757        assert!(
7758            user_unwritable(&mappings, 0x2fc0, 128),
7759            "runs into read-only"
7760        );
7761        assert!(user_unwritable(&mappings, 0x6000, 8), "execute-only");
7762        assert!(
7763            user_unwritable(&mappings, 0x4000, 8),
7764            "a hole below a fixed mapping"
7765        );
7766        assert!(
7767            !user_unwritable(&mappings, 0x8ff8, 16),
7768            "a hole below the grow-down stack, into it"
7769        );
7770        assert!(user_unwritable(&mappings, usize::MAX - 4, 8), "overflow");
7771    }
7772
7773    /// The first byte user mode cannot read is found across contiguous
7774    /// readable, write-only and execute-only mappings, and is a `PROT_NONE`
7775    /// mapping, a hole, or a hole below a grow-down mapping.
7776    #[test]
7777    fn the_first_user_unreadable_byte_is_classified_by_its_mapping() {
7778        let smaps = "1000-2000 r--p 0 0:0 0\nVmFlags: rd mr\n\
7779                     2000-3000 -w-p 0 0:0 0\nVmFlags: wr mw\n\
7780                     3000-4000 ---p 0 0:0 0\nVmFlags: mr\n\
7781                     6000-7000 --xp 0 0:0 0 [vvar]\nVmFlags: ex\n\
7782                     9000-a000 rw-p 0 0:0 0 [stack]\nVmFlags: rd wr gd\n";
7783        let mappings = parse_smaps(smaps);
7784        assert_eq!(mappings.len(), 5);
7785        assert!(mappings[4].growsdown && !mappings[0].growsdown);
7786        assert_eq!(
7787            first_user_unreadable(&mappings, 0x1fc0, 128),
7788            None,
7789            "crosses from read-only into write-only"
7790        );
7791        assert_eq!(
7792            first_user_unreadable(&mappings, 0x6000, 128),
7793            None,
7794            "execute-only"
7795        );
7796        assert_eq!(
7797            first_user_unreadable(&mappings, 0x2fc0, 128),
7798            Some(Unreadable::NoPermission),
7799            "runs into PROT_NONE"
7800        );
7801        assert_eq!(
7802            first_user_unreadable(&mappings, 0x4000, 48),
7803            Some(Unreadable::Hole {
7804                below_growsdown: false
7805            }),
7806            "a hole below a fixed mapping"
7807        );
7808        assert_eq!(
7809            first_user_unreadable(&mappings, 0x8000, 48),
7810            Some(Unreadable::Hole {
7811                below_growsdown: true
7812            }),
7813            "a hole below the stack"
7814        );
7815        assert_eq!(
7816            first_user_unreadable(&mappings, 0xb000, 48),
7817            Some(Unreadable::Hole {
7818                below_growsdown: false
7819            }),
7820            "above every mapping"
7821        );
7822        assert_eq!(
7823            first_user_unreadable(&mappings, 0, 48),
7824            Some(Unreadable::Hole {
7825                below_growsdown: false
7826            })
7827        );
7828        assert_eq!(
7829            first_user_unreadable(&mappings, usize::MAX - 10, 48),
7830            Some(Unreadable::Hole {
7831                below_growsdown: false
7832            })
7833        );
7834    }
7835
7836    #[test]
7837    fn the_sender_named_by_its_call_is_admitted_whatever_its_siblings_masks() {
7838        assert_eq!(refusal(|_| {}), None);
7839    }
7840
7841    /// S2: the call names the leader but another thread sends; admitted only
7842    /// when every other live thread blocks the signal or has its exit granted.
7843    #[test]
7844    fn another_sender_is_admitted_only_when_every_live_sibling_blocks() {
7845        let usr1 = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGUSR1);
7846        let leader = DetTid::from_raw(SENDER_SIBLING);
7847        assert_eq!(
7848            refusal(|send| send.names_sender = false),
7849            Some(OwnProcessSignalRefusal::NotSoleReceiver(leader))
7850        );
7851        assert_eq!(
7852            refusal(|send| {
7853                send.names_sender = false;
7854                send.inputs.siblings = vec![(leader, SiblingSignalState::InSchedule(Some(usr1)))];
7855            }),
7856            None
7857        );
7858        assert_eq!(
7859            refusal(|send| {
7860                send.names_sender = false;
7861                send.inputs.siblings = vec![(leader, SiblingSignalState::ExitGranted)];
7862            }),
7863            None
7864        );
7865    }
7866
7867    /// Only the seven admitted signals pass; each other signal is refused
7868    /// whatever its disposition.
7869    #[test]
7870    fn only_the_seven_signals_below_sigchld_are_admitted() {
7871        for sig in 1..=64 {
7872            let admitted = OWN_PROCESS_SIGNALS.contains(&sig);
7873            let got = refusal(|send| {
7874                send.sig = sig;
7875                send.caught = kernel_sigset_bit(sig);
7876            });
7877            if admitted {
7878                assert!(sig < libc::SIGCHLD, "signal {sig}");
7879                assert_eq!(got, None, "signal {sig}");
7880            } else {
7881                assert_eq!(
7882                    got,
7883                    Some(OwnProcessSignalRefusal::SignalNotAdmitted),
7884                    "signal {sig}"
7885                );
7886            }
7887        }
7888        for sig in [
7889            libc::SIGCHLD,
7890            libc::SIGCONT,
7891            libc::SIGTSTP,
7892            libc::SIGRTMIN(),
7893            libc::SIGSEGV,
7894            libc::SIGTRAP,
7895            libc::SIGSTKFLT,
7896            libc::SIGKILL,
7897            libc::SIGALRM,
7898        ] {
7899            assert!(!OWN_PROCESS_SIGNALS.contains(&sig), "signal {sig}");
7900        }
7901    }
7902
7903    #[test]
7904    fn a_default_disposition_is_refused_and_sig_ign_is_admitted() {
7905        assert_eq!(
7906            refusal(|send| send.caught = 0),
7907            Some(OwnProcessSignalRefusal::DefaultDisposition)
7908        );
7909        assert_eq!(
7910            refusal(|send| {
7911                send.caught = 0;
7912                send.ignored = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGUSR1);
7913            }),
7914            None
7915        );
7916    }
7917
7918    #[test]
7919    fn each_mode_and_sender_state_refuses_by_name() {
7920        use OwnProcessSignalRefusal as R;
7921        let sibling = DetTid::from_raw(SENDER_SIBLING);
7922        assert_eq!(
7923            refusal(|send| send.record_replay = true),
7924            Some(R::RecordReplay)
7925        );
7926        assert_eq!(
7927            refusal(|send| send.schedule_replay = true),
7928            Some(R::ScheduleReplay)
7929        );
7930        assert_eq!(
7931            refusal(|send| send.inputs.guest_marker_signal = true),
7932            Some(R::GuestMarkerSignal)
7933        );
7934        assert_eq!(
7935            refusal(|send| send.stacktrace_signal = true),
7936            Some(R::StacktraceSignal)
7937        );
7938        assert_eq!(
7939            refusal(|send| send.blocked = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGUSR1)),
7940            Some(R::SenderBlocks)
7941        );
7942        assert_eq!(
7943            refusal(|send| send.inputs.siblings[0].1 = SiblingSignalState::OutOfSchedule),
7944            Some(R::OutOfSchedule(sibling))
7945        );
7946        assert_eq!(
7947            refusal(|send| send.inputs.siblings[0].1 = SiblingSignalState::InSchedule(None)),
7948            Some(R::UnknownMask(sibling))
7949        );
7950        assert_eq!(
7951            refusal(|send| send.posthook_anchor = true),
7952            Some(R::PosthookAnchor)
7953        );
7954        assert_eq!(refusal(|send| send.signalfd = true), Some(R::Signalfd));
7955        assert_eq!(
7956            refusal(|send| send.inputs.exec_in_flight = true),
7957            Some(R::ExecInFlight)
7958        );
7959    }
7960
7961    /// The private queue refuses on any signal the sender does not block,
7962    /// except Reverie's timer marker.
7963    #[test]
7964    fn a_private_pending_signal_refuses_unless_blocked_or_the_timer_marker() {
7965        let hup = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGHUP);
7966        let marker = kernel_sigset_bit(reverie::PERF_EVENT_SIGNAL as i32);
7967        assert_eq!(
7968            refusal(|send| send.thread_pending = hup),
7969            Some(OwnProcessSignalRefusal::PrivatePending(hup))
7970        );
7971        assert_eq!(
7972            refusal(|send| {
7973                send.thread_pending = hup;
7974                send.blocked = hup;
7975            }),
7976            None
7977        );
7978        assert_eq!(refusal(|send| send.thread_pending = marker), None);
7979    }
7980
7981    /// An armed host-timed source or a foreign exit signal refuses whatever its
7982    /// number, unless the sender blocks it.
7983    #[test]
7984    fn armed_sources_refuse_unless_the_sender_blocks_them() {
7985        let io = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGIO);
7986        let usr2 = kernel_sigset_bit(libc::SIGUSR2);
7987        assert_eq!(
7988            refusal(|send| send.inputs.host_timed = io),
7989            Some(OwnProcessSignalRefusal::HostTimedSource(io))
7990        );
7991        assert_eq!(
7992            refusal(|send| {
7993                send.inputs.host_timed = io;
7994                send.blocked = io;
7995            }),
7996            None
7997        );
7998        assert_eq!(
7999            refusal(|send| send.inputs.foreign_exit_signals = usr2),
8000            Some(OwnProcessSignalRefusal::ForeignExitSignal(usr2))
8001        );
8002        assert_eq!(
8003            refusal(|send| {
8004                send.inputs.foreign_exit_signals = usr2;
8005                send.blocked = usr2;
8006            }),
8007            None
8008        );
8009    }
8010
8011    /// An undelivered scheduler signal refuses when numbered at or below the
8012    /// one sent, which it would precede or coalesce with, and not above it.
8013    #[test]
8014    fn an_undelivered_scheduler_signal_refuses_only_at_or_below_the_sent_one() {
8015        let term = libc::SIGTERM;
8016        for (pending, refuses) in [
8017            (libc::SIGHUP, true),
8018            (libc::SIGALRM, true),
8019            (libc::SIGTERM, true),
8020            (libc::SIGSTKFLT, false),
8021            (libc::SIGRTMIN(), false),
8022        ] {
8023            let bit = kernel_sigset_bit(pending);
8024            let got = refusal(|send| {
8025                send.sig = term;
8026                send.caught = kernel_sigset_bit(term);
8027                send.inputs.undelivered_scheduler_signals = bit;
8028            });
8029            assert_eq!(
8030                got,
8031                refuses.then_some(OwnProcessSignalRefusal::UndeliveredSchedulerSignal(bit)),
8032                "pending {pending}"
8033            );
8034        }
8035    }
8036}
